Appendix E
The intermediate stages of the WWW symmetry breaking pattern

Schematically, we assign the Higgs VEVs according to the symmetry breaking pattern as follows:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{351}\to\mathfrak{g}_{341}\penalty\

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle\penalty\ \langle(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{5}}\,,-\frac{1}{5})_{\mathbf{H}\,,{\rm IV}}\rangle\equiv\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}W_{\mathbf{\overline{5}}\,,{\rm IV}}\,,

(188a)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{341}\to\mathfrak{g}_{331}\penalty\

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle\penalty\ \langle(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,-\frac{1}{4})_{\mathbf{H}\,,{\rm V}}\rangle\equiv\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,{\rm V}}\,,\penalty\ \langle(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,-\frac{1}{4})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{1},\dot{\rm VII}}\rangle\equiv\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1},\dot{\rm VII}}\,,

(188b)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{331}\to\mathfrak{g}_{\rm SM}\penalty\

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle\penalty\ \langle(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,-\frac{1}{3})_{\mathbf{H}\,,3,\rm{VI}}\rangle\equiv\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}V_{\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,3,\rm{VI}}\,,

(188c)

\displaystyle\langle(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,-\frac{1}{3})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\rm VIII}}\rangle\equiv\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}V_{\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,\dot{\rm VIII}}\,,\penalty\ \langle(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,-\frac{1}{3})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{2},\dot{\rm IX}}^{\prime}\rangle\equiv\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}V_{\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,\dot{2},\dot{\rm IX}}^{\prime}\,,

\displaystyle{\rm EWSB}\penalty\

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle\penalty\ \langle(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{2}}\,,+\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}}\rangle\equiv\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}v_{\rm EW}\,.

(188d)

For later convenience, we also parametrize different symmetry breaking VEVs in terms of the following dimensionless quantities:

\displaystyle\zeta_{1}\equiv\frac{W_{\mathbf{\overline{5}}\,,{\rm IV}}}{M_{\rm pl}}\,,\quad\zeta_{2}\equiv\frac{w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,{\rm V}}}{M_{\rm pl}}\,,\quad\dot{\zeta}_{2}\equiv\frac{w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1},\dot{\rm VII}}}{M_{\rm pl}}\,,

\displaystyle\zeta_{3}\equiv\frac{V_{\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,3,{\rm VI}}}{M_{\rm pl}}\,,\quad\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{\prime}\equiv\frac{V_{\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,\dot{2},\dot{\rm IX}}^{\prime}}{M_{\rm pl}}\,,\quad\dot{\zeta}_{3}\equiv\frac{V_{\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,\dot{\rm VIII}}}{M_{\rm pl}}\,,

\displaystyle\zeta_{0}\gg\zeta_{1}\gg\zeta_{2}\sim\dot{\zeta}_{2}\gg\zeta_{3}\sim\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{\prime}\sim\dot{\zeta}_{3}\,,\quad\zeta_{ij}\equiv\frac{\zeta_{j}}{\zeta_{i}}\,,\quad(i<j)\,.

(189)

In Table
31
, we summarize all vectorlike fermions that become massive during different stages of the WWW symmetry breaking pattern.

\displaystyle\frac{d_{\mathscr{B}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{\rm VII}}}{2M_{\rm pl}}(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{6}}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\omega}_{1}}^{\dagger}\otimes(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,-\frac{1}{4})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\omega}_{2}}^{\dagger}\otimes(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,+\frac{3}{4})_{\mathbf{H}}^{\dagger}+H.c.

\displaystyle\supset

\displaystyle\frac{Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{\rm VII}}}{2M_{\rm pl}m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{6}}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\omega}_{1}}}^{2}}\Big[(\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,\mathbf{1}\,,+\frac{1}{3})_{\mathbf{F}}^{\dot{\omega}_{1}}\otimes(\mathbf{3}\,,\mathbf{3}\,,0)_{\mathbf{F}}^{\prime\prime}\oplus(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,-\frac{1}{3})_{\mathbf{F}}^{\dot{\omega}_{1}}\otimes(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,+\frac{1}{3})_{\mathbf{F}}^{\prime}\Big]

\displaystyle\otimes

\displaystyle\langle(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,-\frac{1}{3})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\omega}_{2}}^{\prime}\rangle^{\dagger}\otimes(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{3}}\,,+\frac{2}{3})_{\mathbf{H}}^{\dagger}+H.c.

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\frac{Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}}{4}\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{\prime}\Big[\frac{w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{\rm VII}}}{m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{6}}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{1}}}^{2}}\left(s_{L}{d_{R}}^{c}+e_{L}\mu_{R}^{c}\right)+\frac{w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{\rm VII}}}{m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{6}}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{2}}}^{2}}\left(s_{L}{s_{R}}^{c}+\mu_{L}\mu_{R}^{c}\right)\Big]v_{\rm EW}+H.c.\,,

via the Higgs mixing operator of
\mathcal{O}_{\mathscr{B}}^{d=5}
in Eq. (
8b
).
For convenience, we parametrize the following ratios of

\displaystyle\Delta_{\dot{\omega}}\equiv\frac{w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{\rm VII}}}{m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,-\frac{1}{4})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\omega}}}^{2}}\,,\quad\Delta_{\dot{\omega}}^{\prime}\equiv\frac{w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{\rm VII}}}{m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{6}}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\omega}}}^{2}}\,.

(206)

With the Higgs VEVs assignments in Eq. (
188
), we can obtain the following propagator masses of

\displaystyle m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,-\frac{1}{4})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{1}}}\sim m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{6}}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{1}}}\sim\mathcal{O}(v_{341})\,,

\displaystyle m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,-\frac{1}{4})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{2}}}\sim m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{\overline{6}}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{2}}}\sim\mathcal{O}(v_{331})\,.

(207)

E.6
The SM quark/lepton masses and the benchmark

For all up-type quarks with
Q_{e}=+\frac{2}{3}
, we write down the following tree-level masses from both the renormalizable Yukawa couplings and the gravity-induced terms in the basis of
\mathcal{U}\equiv(u\,,c\,,t\,)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{\mathcal{U}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&c_{4}\dot{\zeta}_{2}/\sqrt{2}&c_{5}\zeta_{1}/\sqrt{2}\\
c_{4}\dot{\zeta}_{2}/\sqrt{2}&0&c_{5}\zeta_{2}/\sqrt{2}\\
c_{5}\zeta_{1}/\sqrt{2}&c_{5}\zeta_{2}/\sqrt{2}&Y_{\mathcal{T}}\\
\end{array}\right)v_{\rm EW}\,.

For all down-type quarks with
Q_{e}=-\frac{1}{3}
, we find the following tree-level mass matrix in the basis of
\mathcal{D}\equiv(d\,,s\,,b\,)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{\mathcal{D}}\approx\frac{1}{4}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\Delta_{\dot{1}}\dot{\zeta}_{3}&Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\Delta_{\dot{2}}\dot{\zeta}_{3}&0\\
Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\Delta_{\dot{1}}^{\prime}\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{\prime}&Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\Delta_{\dot{2}}^{\prime}\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{\prime}&0\\
0&0&Y_{\mathcal{B}}d_{\mathscr{A}}\zeta_{23}^{-1}\zeta_{1}\\
\end{array}\right)v_{\rm EW}\,,

where we interpret the ratio between two
\mathfrak{g}_{331}
VEVs as the Cabibbo angle of
\tan\lambda\equiv\frac{\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{\prime}}{\dot{\zeta}_{3}}
.
For all charged leptons with
Q_{e}=-1
, their tree-level mass matrix is related to the down-type quark mass matrix as
\mathcal{M}_{\mathcal{L}}=\mathcal{M}^{T}_{\mathcal{D}}
.
Based on the above SM quark/lepton mass matrices, we find the following benchmark point of

\displaystyle v_{351}\simeq 1.4\times 10^{17}\,\mathrm{GeV}\,,\quad v_{341}\simeq 4.8\times 10^{15}\,\mathrm{GeV}\,,\quad v_{331}\simeq 4.8\times 10^{13}\,\mathrm{GeV}\,,\quad

(216)

to reproduce the observed hierarchical masses as well as the CKM mixing pattern, as summarized in Table
35
.
